namespace = mbs_prophecy

################################
#Vision of Pregnancy - Your Vision
long_character_event = {
	id = mbs_prophecy.1
	title = "EVTtitlembs_prophecy.1"
	desc = "EVTDESCmbs_prophecy.1"
	picture = "GFX_green_dream"
	
	only_rulers = no
	only_capable = yes
	min_age = 16
	is_married = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		is_ruler = yes
		prisoner = no
		NOT = { trait = infirm }
		is_incapable = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		age = 16
		NOT = { age = 60 }
		OR = {
			trait = mystic
			trait = dragon_dreams
			trait = greensight
			trait = greenseer
			trait = cotf
		}
		AND = {
			is_married = yes
			is_pregnant = no
			NOT = {
				trait = celibate
			}
			NOT = {
				trait = eunuch
			}
			spouse = {
				prisoner = no
				is_alive = yes
				is_abroad = no
				NOT = { trait = infirm }
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				age = 16
				NOT = { age = 60 }
				NOT = {
					trait = celibate
				}
				NOT = {
					trait = eunuch
				}
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
				reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
				is_pregnant = no
			}
		}
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.9
			fertility >= 0.7
		}
	}

	
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.1" #
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { 
					spouse = { is_female = yes }
					is_female = no 
				}
				spouse = {
					impregnate = ROOT
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { 
					spouse = { is_female = no }
					is_female = yes
				}
				ROOT = { impregnate = spouse }	
			}
		}
	}
}
#Vision of Pregnancy - Someone Else's Vision
long_character_event = {
	id = mbs_prophecy.2
	title = "EVTtitlembs_prophecy.2"
	desc = "EVTDESCmbs_prophecy.2"
	picture = "GFX_green_dream"
	
	only_rulers = no
	only_capable = yes
	min_age = 16
	is_married = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		is_ruler = yes
		prisoner = no
		NOT = { trait = infirm }
		is_incapable = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		age = 16
		NOT = { age = 60 }
		OR = {
			any_close_relative = {
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			any_courtier = {
				has_minor_title = title_hedgemage_mbs
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		AND = {
			is_married = yes
			is_pregnant = no
			NOT = {
				trait = celibate
			}
			NOT = {
				trait = eunuch
			}
			spouse = {
				prisoner = no
				is_alive = yes
				is_abroad = no
				NOT = { trait = infirm }
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				age = 16
				NOT = { age = 60 }
				NOT = {
					trait = celibate
				}
				NOT = {
					trait = eunuch
				}
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
				reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
				is_pregnant = no
			}
		}
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.9
			fertility >= 0.7
		}
	}

	
	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					has_minor_title = title_hedgemage_mbs
				}
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			save_event_target_as = mbs_prophecygiver
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.2" #
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { 
					spouse = { is_female = yes }
					is_female = no 
				}
				spouse = {
					impregnate = ROOT
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { 
					spouse = { is_female = no }
					is_female = yes
				}
				ROOT = { impregnate = spouse }	
			}
		}
	}
	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clear_event_target = mbs_prophecygiver
		}
	}
}
#Vision of Twins - Your Vision
long_character_event = {
	id = mbs_prophecy.3
	title = "EVTtitlembs_prophecy.3"
	desc = "EVTDESCmbs_prophecy.3"
	picture = "GFX_green_dream"
	
	only_rulers = no
	only_capable = yes
	min_age = 16
	is_married = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		is_ruler = yes
		prisoner = no
		NOT = { trait = infirm }
		is_incapable = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		age = 16
		NOT = { age = 60 }
		OR = {
			trait = mystic
			trait = dragon_dreams
			trait = greensight
			trait = greenseer
			trait = cotf
		}
		AND = {
			is_married = yes
			is_pregnant = no
			NOT = {
				trait = celibate
			}
			NOT = {
				trait = eunuch
			}
			spouse = {
				prisoner = no
				is_alive = yes
				is_abroad = no
				NOT = { trait = infirm }
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				age = 16
				NOT = { age = 60 }
				NOT = {
					trait = celibate
				}
				NOT = {
					trait = eunuch
				}
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
				reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
				is_pregnant = no
			}
		}
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.9
			fertility >= 0.7
		}
	}

	
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.3" #
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { 
					spouse = { is_female = yes }
					is_female = no 
				}
				spouse = {
					unsafe_impregnate = ROOT
					unsafe_impregnate = ROOT
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { 
					spouse = { is_female = no }
					is_female = yes
				}
				ROOT = { unsafe_impregnate = spouse }	
				ROOT = { unsafe_impregnate = spouse }	
			}
		}
	}
}

long_character_event = {
	id = mbs_prophecy.5
	title = "EVTtitlembs_prophecy.5"
	desc = "EVTDESCmbs_prophecy.5"
	picture = "GFX_green_dream"
	
	only_rulers = no
	only_capable = yes
	min_age = 16
	is_married = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		is_ruler = yes
		prisoner = no
		NOT = { trait = infirm }
		is_incapable = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		age = 16
		NOT = { age = 60 }
		OR = {
			trait = mystic
			trait = dragon_dreams
			trait = greensight
			trait = greenseer
			trait = cotf
		}
		AND = {
			is_married = yes
			is_pregnant = no
			NOT = {
				trait = celibate
			}
			NOT = {
				trait = eunuch
			}
			spouse = {
				prisoner = no
				is_alive = yes
				is_abroad = no
				NOT = { trait = infirm }
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				age = 16
				NOT = { age = 60 }
				NOT = {
					trait = celibate
				}
				NOT = {
					trait = eunuch
				}
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
				reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
				is_pregnant = no
			}
		}
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.9
			fertility >= 0.7
		}
	}

	
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.5" #
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { 
					spouse = { is_female = yes }
					is_female = no 
				}
				spouse = {
					impregnate = ROOT
				}
				spouse = {
					set_character_flag = mbs_glorious_pregnancy
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { 
					spouse = { is_female = no }
					is_female = yes
				}
				ROOT = { impregnate = spouse }	
				set_character_flag = mbs_glorious_pregnancy
			}
		}
	}
}

			save_event_target_as = mbs_prophecygiver
		}
	}

	option = {

					is_female = no 
				}
				spouse = {
					impregnate = ROOT
				}
				spouse = {

				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { 
					spouse = { is_female = no }
					is_female = yes
				}
				ROOT = { impregnate = spouse }	
				set_character_flag = mbs_deformity_pregnancy
			}
		}

#Vision of a child's beautiful destiny - Someone Else's Vision
long_character_event = {
	id = mbs_prophecy.30
	title = "EVTtitlembs_prophecy.30"
	desc = "EVTDESCmbs_prophecy.30"
	picture = "GFX_green_dream"
	
	only_rulers = no
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		is_ruler = yes
		prisoner = no
		is_incapable = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		age = 16
		OR = {
			any_close_relative = {
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			any_courtier = {
				has_minor_title = title_hedgemage_mbs
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		any_child = {
			NOT = { age = 6 }
			is_incapable = no
			NOT = { NOT_mythical_creature_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			NOR = {
				has_character_flag = mbs_glorious_child
				has_character_flag = mbs_martial_child
				has_character_flag = mbs_scholarly_child
				has_character_flag = mbs_beauty_child

				has_character_flag = mbs_wastrel_child
				has_character_flag = mbs_schemer_child

				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_siblings
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_goldenchild
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_goldenchildwithflaws
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_septonmaester
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_shutin
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_wildchild
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_rogue
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_wastrel
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_brightflame
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_poet
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_sombre
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_softhearted
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_brute
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_hardnosed
			}
		}
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					has_minor_title = title_hedgemage_mbs
				}				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			save_event_target_as = mbs_prophecygiver
		}
	}

	
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.30" #
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_child = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { age = 6 }
					is_incapable = no
					NOT = { NOT_mythical_creature_trigger = yes }

						has_character_flag = mbs_martial_child
						has_character_flag = mbs_scholarly_child
						has_character_flag = mbs_beauty_child
						has_character_flag = mbs_gifted_rule_child
						has_character_flag = mbs_wastrel_child
						has_character_flag = mbs_schemer_child
						has_character_flag = mbs_cruel_child
						has_character_flag = blessed_targ_siblings
						has_character_flag = blessed_targ_goldenchild
						has_character_flag = blessed_targ_goldenchildwithflaws
						has_character_flag = blessed_targ_septonmaester
						has_character_flag = blessed_targ_shutin
						has_character_flag = blessed_targ_wildchild
						has_character_flag = blessed_targ_rogue
						has_character_flag = blessed_targ_wastrel
						has_character_flag = blessed_targ_brightflame

	id = mbs_prophecy.36
	title = "EVTtitlembs_prophecy.36"
	desc = "EVTDESCmbs_prophecy.36"
	picture = "GFX_green_dream"
	
	only_rulers = no
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		is_ruler = yes
		prisoner = no
		is_incapable = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		age = 16
		OR = {
			any_close_relative = {
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			any_courtier = {
				has_minor_title = title_hedgemage_mbs
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		any_child = {
			NOT = { age = 6 }
			is_incapable = no
			NOT = { NOT_mythical_creature_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			NOR = {
				has_character_flag = mbs_glorious_child
				has_character_flag = mbs_martial_child
				has_character_flag = mbs_scholarly_child
				has_character_flag = mbs_beauty_child
				has_character_flag = mbs_gifted_rule_child
				has_character_flag = mbs_wastrel_child
				has_character_flag = mbs_schemer_child
				has_character_flag = mbs_cruel_child
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_siblings
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_goldenchild
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_goldenchildwithflaws
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_septonmaester
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_shutin
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_wildchild
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_rogue
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_wastrel
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_brightflame
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_poet
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_sombre
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_softhearted
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_brute
				has_character_flag = blessed_targ_hardnosed
			}
		}
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					has_minor_title = title_hedgemage_mbs
				}				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			save_event_target_as = mbs_prophecygiver
		}
	}

	
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.36" #
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_child = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { age = 6 }
					is_incapable = no

						has_character_flag = mbs_martial_child
						has_character_flag = mbs_scholarly_child

						has_character_flag = mbs_gifted_rule_child
						has_character_flag = mbs_wastrel_child
						has_character_flag = mbs_schemer_child
						has_character_flag = mbs_cruel_child
						has_character_flag = blessed_targ_siblings
						has_character_flag = blessed_targ_goldenchild
						has_character_flag = blessed_targ_goldenchildwithflaws

long_character_event = {
	id = mbs_prophecy.39
	title = "EVTtitlembs_prophecy.39"
	desc = "EVTDESCmbs_prophecy.39"
	picture = "GFX_green_dream"
	
	only_rulers = no
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		is_ruler = yes
		prisoner = no
		is_incapable = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		age = 16
		OR = {
			trait = mystic
			trait = dragon_dreams
			trait = greensight
			trait = greenseer
			trait = cotf
		}
		any_child = {
			NOT = { age = 15 }
			is_incapable = no
			NOT = { NOT_mythical_creature_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		}
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250
	}

	
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.39" #
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_child = {
				limit = {
					NOT = { age = 15 }
					is_incapable = no
					NOT = { NOT_mythical_creature_trigger = yes }
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				}
				add_trait = fever
				add_trait = fatigue
				add_trait = ill
				set_character_flag = developing_illness
				random_list = {
					10 = {
						add_trait = has_tuberculosis
					}
					10 = {
						add_trait = dysentery
					}
					10 = {
						add_trait = flu
					}
					10 = {
						add_trait = pneumonic
					}
					10 = {
						add_trait = cancer
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		is_ruler = yes
		prisoner = no
		is_incapable = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		age = 16
		OR = {
			trait = mystic
			trait = dragon_dreams
			trait = greensight
			trait = greenseer
			trait = cotf
		}
		any_dynasty_member = {
			age = 15
			at_location = ROOT
			is_incapable = no
			NOT = { NOT_mythical_creature_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		}
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250
	}

	
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.41" #
		hidden_tooltip = {
			random_dynasty_member = {
				limit = {
					age = 15
					is_incapable = no
					NOT = { NOT_mythical_creature_trigger = yes }
					NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
					at_location = ROOT
				}
				add_trait = fever
				add_trait = fatigue
				add_trait = ill
				set_character_flag = developing_illness
				random_list = {
					10 = {
						add_trait = has_tuberculosis
					}
					10 = {
						add_trait = dysentery
					}
					10 = {
						add_trait = flu
					}
					10 = {
						add_trait = pneumonic
					}
					10 = {
						add_trait = cancer
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

	id = mbs_prophecy.42

	only_rulers = no
	only_capable = yes

#Vision of strife between kin - Your Vision
long_character_event = {
	id = mbs_prophecy.43
	title = "EVTtitlembs_prophecy.43"
	desc = "EVTDESCmbs_prophecy.43"
	picture = "GFX_green_dream"
	
	only_rulers = no
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		is_ruler = yes
		prisoner = no
		is_incapable = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		age = 16
		any_dynasty_member = {
			count = 2
			NOT = { character = ROOT }
			age = 15
			at_location = ROOT
			prisoner = no
			is_incapable = no
			NOT = { NOT_mythical_creature_trigger = yes }
			NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		}
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250
	}

	immediate = {
		random_dynasty_member = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = prophecy_rival_1 }
				NOT = { has_character_flag = prophecy_rival_2 }
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				age = 15
				at_location = ROOT
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { NOT_mythical_creature_trigger = yes }
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			save_event_target_as = prophecy_rival_1
			set_character_flag = prophecy_rival_1
		}
		random_dynasty_member = {
			limit = {
				NOT = { has_character_flag = prophecy_rival_1 }
				NOT = { has_character_flag = prophecy_rival_2 }
				NOT = { character = ROOT }
				age = 15
				at_location = ROOT
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { NOT_mythical_creature_trigger = yes }
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			save_event_target_as = prophecy_rival_2
			set_character_flag = prophecy_rival_2
		}
	}	

	
	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.43
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:prophecy_rival_1 = {
				add_rival = prophecy_rival_2
			}
			event_target:prophecy_rival_2 = {
				add_rival = prophecy_rival_1
			}
		}
	}
	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			event_target:prophecy_rival_1 = { clr_character_flag = prophecy_rival_1 }
			event_target:prophecy_rival_2 = { clr_character_flag = prophecy_rival_2 }
			clear_event_target = prophecy_rival_1
			clear_event_target = prophecy_rival_2
		}
	}
}

					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf

			has_landed_title = k_vale

					region = world_vale

					trait = cotf

	picture = "GFX_green_dream"

	only_rulers = no
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		is_ruler = yes
		prisoner = no
		is_incapable = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		OR = {
			any_close_relative = {
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			any_courtier = {
				has_minor_title = title_hedgemage_mbs
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_landed_title = e_riverlands
			has_landed_title = k_riverlands
			has_landed_title = e_vale
			has_landed_title = k_vale
			capital_scope = {
				OR = {
					region = world_riverlands
					region = world_vale
				}
			}
			culture = riverlander
			culture = valeman
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					has_minor_title = title_hedgemage_mbs
				}				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			save_event_target_as = mbs_prophecygiver
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.94
	}

	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clear_event_target = mbs_prophecygiver
		}
	}
}

	id = mbs_prophecy.108
	title = "EVTtitlembs_prophecy.108"
	desc = "EVTDESCmbs_prophecy.108"
	picture = "GFX_green_dream"

	only_rulers = no
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		is_ruler = yes
		prisoner = no
		is_incapable = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		OR = {
			any_close_relative = {
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			any_courtier = {
				has_minor_title = title_hedgemage_mbs
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_landed_title = e_riverlands
			has_landed_title = k_riverlands
			has_landed_title = e_dorne
			has_landed_title = k_dorne
			capital_scope = {
				OR = {
					region = world_riverlands
					region = world_dorne
				}
			}
			culture = riverlander
			culture = sand_dornish
			culture = salt_dornish
			culture = stone_dornish
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					has_minor_title = title_hedgemage_mbs
				}				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			save_event_target_as = mbs_prophecygiver
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.108
	}

	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clear_event_target = mbs_prophecygiver
		}
	}
}

					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_landed_title = e_westerlands
			has_landed_title = k_westerlands
			has_landed_title = e_dorne
			has_landed_title = k_dorne

					region = world_dorne
				}
			}
			culture = westerman

					is_close_relative = ROOT

					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			any_courtier = {
				has_minor_title = title_hedgemage_mbs
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_landed_title = e_dorne
			has_landed_title = k_dorne
			has_landed_title = e_iron_isles
			has_landed_title = k_iron_isles
			capital_scope = {
				OR = {
					region = world_dorne
					region = world_iron_isles
				}
			}
			culture = sand_dornish
			culture = salt_dornish
			culture = stone_dornish
			culture = ironborn
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					has_minor_title = title_hedgemage_mbs
				}				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			save_event_target_as = mbs_prophecygiver
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.172
	}

	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clear_event_target = mbs_prophecygiver
		}
	}
}

long_character_event = {
	id = mbs_prophecy.174
	title = "EVTtitlembs_prophecy.174"
	desc = "EVTDESCmbs_prophecy.174"
	picture = "GFX_green_dream"

	only_rulers = no
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		is_ruler = yes
		prisoner = no
		is_incapable = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		OR = {
			any_close_relative = {
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			any_courtier = {
				has_minor_title = title_hedgemage_mbs
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			has_landed_title = e_dorne
			has_landed_title = k_dorne
			has_landed_title = e_iron_isles
			has_landed_title = k_iron_isles
			capital_scope = {
				OR = {
					region = world_dorne
					region = world_iron_isles
				}
			}
			culture = sand_dornish
			culture = salt_dornish
			culture = stone_dornish
			culture = ironborn
		}
	}

	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_close_relative = ROOT

				}				OR = {

		}
	}

		}
		modifier = {

		}

		}

		}
		modifier = {

		}
		modifier = {

#Gain seducer / seductress
character_event = {
	id = mbs_prophecy.208
	desc = "EVTDESCmbs_prophecy.208"
	picture = "GFX_evt_courtiers_talking"

	is_triggered_only = yes

	trigger = {
		age = 16
		has_character_flag = mbs_schemer_child
		OR = {
			AND = {
				is_female = no
				NOT = { trait = seducer }
			}
			AND = {
				is_female = yes
				NOT = { trait = seductress }
			}
		}
	}

	weight_multiplier = {
		modifier = {
			factor = 3
			trait = lustful
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.50
			has_attractive_trait_trigger = yes
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.25
			trait = hedonist
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.20
			trait = deceitful
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.20
			trait = schemer
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.15
			trait = ambitious
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.15
			trait = gregarious
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.10
			trait = groomed
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.10
			trait = fashionable
		}
		modifier = {
			factor = 1.05
			trait = selfish
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.208"

		trigger = {
			is_female = no
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 2
		}

		add_trait = seducer
	}

	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.208"

		trigger = {
			is_female = yes
		}

		ai_chance = {
			factor = 2
		}

		add_trait = seductress
	}
}

#Vision of shadows
long_character_event = {
	id = mbs_prophecy.213
	title = "EVTtitlembs_prophecy.213"
	desc = "EVTDESCmbs_prophecy.213"
	picture = "GFX_green_dream"
	
	only_rulers = no
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		is_ruler = yes
		prisoner = no
		is_incapable = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.213
	}
}
#Vision of shadows - Someone Else's Vision
long_character_event = {
	id = mbs_prophecy.214
	title = "EVTtitlembs_prophecy.214"
	desc = "EVTDESCmbs_prophecy.214"
	picture = "GFX_green_dream"
	
	only_rulers = no
	only_capable = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	trigger = {
		ai = no
		is_ruler = yes
		prisoner = no
		is_incapable = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		OR = {
			any_close_relative = {
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			any_courtier = {
				has_minor_title = title_hedgemage_mbs
				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
		}
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250
	}

	immediate = {
		random_courtier = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_close_relative = ROOT
					has_minor_title = title_hedgemage_mbs
				}				OR = {
					trait = mystic
					trait = dragon_dreams
					trait = greensight
					trait = greenseer
					trait = cotf
				}
				prisoner = no
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
			}
			save_event_target_as = mbs_prophecygiver
		}
	}

	option = {
		name = EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.214
	}

	after = {
		hidden_tooltip = {
			clear_event_target = mbs_prophecygiver
		}
	}
}

#Vision of Triplets - Your Vision
long_character_event = {
	id = mbs_prophecy.221
	title = "EVTtitlembs_prophecy.221"
	desc = "EVTDESCmbs_prophecy.221"
	picture = "GFX_green_dream"
	
	only_rulers = no
	only_capable = yes
	min_age = 16
	is_married = yes
	prisoner = no
	capable_only = yes

	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
		is_ruler = yes
		prisoner = no
		NOT = { trait = infirm }
		is_incapable = no
		NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
		age = 16
		NOT = { age = 60 }
		OR = {
			trait = mystic
			trait = dragon_dreams
			trait = greensight
			trait = greenseer
			trait = cotf
		}
		AND = {
			is_married = yes
			is_pregnant = no
			NOT = {
				trait = celibate
			}
			NOT = {
				trait = eunuch
			}
			spouse = {
				prisoner = no
				is_alive = yes
				is_abroad = no
				NOT = { trait = infirm }
				is_incapable = no
				NOT = { is_inaccessible_trigger = yes }
				age = 16
				NOT = { age = 60 }
				NOT = {
					trait = celibate
				}
				NOT = {
					trait = eunuch
				}
				opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
				reverse_opinion = { who = ROOT value = 20 }
				is_pregnant = no
			}
		}
	}
	
	mean_time_to_happen = {
		years = 250

		modifier = {
			factor = 0.9
			fertility >= 0.7
		}
	}

	
	option = {
		name = "EVTOPTAmbs_prophecy.221" #
		hidden_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { 
					spouse = { is_female = yes }
					is_female = no 
				}
				spouse = {
					unsafe_impregnate = ROOT
					unsafe_impregnate = ROOT
					unsafe_impregnate = ROOT
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { 
					spouse = { is_female = no }
					is_female = yes
				}
				ROOT = { unsafe_impregnate = spouse }	
				ROOT = { unsafe_impregnate = spouse }	
				ROOT = { unsafe_impregnate = spouse }	
			}
		}
	}
}
